No.  CWr{1) /73r — ^Thc  follovVmg  draft  of  certain 
amendments  to  the  Cost  and  Works  Acloountants  Regu- 
lations, 1959,  proposed  to  be  made  in  exercise  of  the 
powers  conferred  by  sub-sections  (1)  and  (3)  of  Section 
39  pf  the  Cost  and  Works  Accountants  Act,  1959  (Act 
Number  23  of  1959),  is  published  for  information  of  all 
persons  likely  to  be  affected  thereby  and  notice  is  hereby 
given  that  the  draft  will  be  taken  up  for  considera^'on 
on  or  after  25th  May  1973. 

Any  objection  or  suggestion  which  may  be  received 
from  any  persons  with  respect  to  the  said  draft  before 
the  date  specified  will  be  considered  by  the  Council  of 
the  Institute  of  Cost  and  Works  Accountants  of  India. 

In  the  said  Regulations  : 

I.  After  the  end  of  Regulation  15  and  before  the  begin- 
ning of  Regulation.  16,  the  following  new  Regulation  15A 
shall  be  inserted,  namely, 

“15A.  Procedure  in  hearing  before  the  Council  : — (1) 
If  the  Council,  on  recording  a finding,  as  required  under 
subHscction  (3)  of  Section  21,  is  of  the  opinion  that 
there  is  a case  for  passing  one  of  the  o!rdcrs  specified 
in  claxise  (a)  or  clause  (b)  of  sub-scction  (4)  of  the 
said  Setion,  it  shall — 

(^a)  furnish  to  the  member  against  whom  the  com- 
plaint  relating  to  the  finding  has  been  made,  a copy  of 
the  report  of  the  Disciplinary  Committee  and  a copy  of 
its  finding;  and 

(b)  give  him  a notice  of  not  less  than  fifteen  days 
indicating  the  order  proposed  to  be  passed  against  him 
and  cjalling  upon  him  to  appear  before  it  on  a specified 
date  or  dates,  or  if  he  does  not  wish  to  be  heatd  in 
person,  to  send  within  a specified  time  such  representa^ 
tion  in  writing  as  he  may  wish  to  make  against  the  prO' 
posed  order. 

(2)  The  scope  of  the  hearing  or  of  the  representation 
in  writing,  as  tne  case  may  be,  shall  be  restricted  to  the 
matters  concerning  the  enquiry. 

(3)  The  Council  shall  after* hearing  the  member  con- 
cerned, if  he  appears  in  person,  or  after  considering  the 
representation,  if  any,  made  by  him,  pass  such  orders 
as  it  may  think  fit, 

(4)  The  orders  passed  by  the  Council  shall  be  com- 
municated to  the  complainant  and  to  the  member  con- 
ceimed.” 

IT.  In  siib-regulation  (1)  of  Regulation  32  and  in  sub- 
regulation  (1)  of  Regulation  35,  for  the  words  and  figure 
“July  1974”,  the  words  and  figure  “June  1974”  shall  be 
substituted. 

III.  In  sub-regulation  (4)  of  Regulation  32A,  and  in 
sub-regulation  (2)  of  Regulation  35 A,  in  the  respecitivo 
proviso  thereto,  for  the  words  “July  1974“  the  words 
“June  1974”  shall  be  substituted. 

TV.  Tn  sub-segulation  (2)  of  Regulation  131,  the  words 
“the  annual  subsdriptions  transferred  to  it  under  Regula- 
tion 7(6)  and”  shall  be  deleted. 

ExpUinatory  Note  on  the  above  Aniendments 

The  following  is  the  Explanatory  Note  on  the  amend- 
ments proposed  in  Notification  No.  CWR(l)/73  dated 
23rd  March  1973.  This  note  is  intended  only  to  clarify 
the  intention  of  the  Council  underlying  these  amend- 


ments and  should  not  be  construed  as  limiting  or  ampli^ 
fying  the  scope  of  these  amendments  in  any  mannei 
whatsoever. 

Item  No.  L — Section  21(4)  of  the  Act  provides  that 
where  the  Council’s  finding  in  a disciplinary  case  ag^nst 
a member  is  that  the  membeir  concerned  is  guilty^  of 
misconduct  specified  in  the  First  Schedule,  the  Council 
shall  afford  to  the  member  an  opportunity  of  being  heard 
before  the  orders  arc  passed  against  him.  The  Council 
has  decided  to  add  a new  Regulation  15A  providing  for 
detailed  procedure  to  be  adopted  in  hearing  before  the 
Council 

Items  II  and  III. — ^The  Council  has  decided  to  hold 
the  Inermediate,  Final  and  Management  Accountancy 
Examinations  in  June  and  T>cciember  of  every  year  in- 
stead of  in  July  and  January  respectively.  The  amend- 
ments proposed  are  consequential  to  this  decision. 

Item  No.  IV, — ^With  the  discontinuation  of  separate 
“Regional  Council  Fee”,  the  reference  to  the  regional 
Council’s  Annual  Subscription  in  Regulation  131(2)  is 
proposed  to  be  deleted. 

No,  C/ 180(2)/ 3/ 73. — In  exercise  of  the  powers  con- 
ferred by  clause  (2)  of  Part  II  of  the  Second  Schedule  to 
the  Cost  and  Works  Accountants  Act,  1959,  (Act  No.  23 
of  1959),  the  Council  of  the  Institute  of  Cost  and  Works 
Accountants  of  India  directs  that  the  following  Clause 
(f)  shall  be  added,  at  the  end,  to  the  Notification  No. 
G/ 180(1) /1 2/ 69,  dated  10th  Dec|ember  1969  issued  by 
the  said  Council : 

“(f)  is  an  empolyee  of  any  of  the  partners  of  a 
firm  of  Chartered  Accountants  who  is/ are  audi- 
tor (s)  of  the  company  appointed  under  Section  224 
of  the  Companies  Act,  1956.” 

S.  N.  GHOSE 

Secretary 

EMPLOYEES’  STATE  INSURANCE  CORPORATION 

New  Delhi y the  5th  April  1973 

No.  Genl/ Amend/ 35, — In  exercise  of  the  powers  con- 
feiired  by  Section  97  of  the  Employees’  State  Insurance 
Act,  1948  (34  of  1948),  the  Employees’  State  Insurance 
Corporation  hereby  makes  the  following  amendment  in 
the  Employees’  State  Insurance  (General)  Regulations 
1950,  the  same  having  been  previously  published  in  the 
Gazette  of  India,  dated  10-6-1972  inviting  suggestio^/ 
objections/ comments  if  any  as  required  by  Sub-section 
(1)  of  the  said  section  namely; — - 
Amendment  to  Regulation  10(1)  of  the  Employees'  State 
Insurance  {General)  Regulations ^ 1950 

The  following  clause  (lA)  shall  be  added  after  clause 
(1)  of  Regulation  10  of  the  Employees’  State  Insurance 
(General)  Regulations,  1950. 

“(lA)  If  a member  Representing  employers/em- 
ployees under  sub-clause  (e)  of  clause  (1)  and  the 
proviso  to  Regulation  lOG),  nominated  by  the 
Chairman  of  the  Corporation,  is  unable  to  attend 
any  meeting  of  the  Regional  Board,  he  may,  by  a 
written  instrument  signed  by  him  and  addressed 
and  delivered  to  the  Chairman  of  the  Regional 
Board  at  least  seven  days  before  the  date  fixed  for 
the  meeting,  explaining  the  reasons  foR  his  inability 
to  attend  the  meeting,  appoint  another  representative 
of  the  Organisation  whiejh  he  represents  on  the 
Regional  Board,  as  his  substitute  for  attending  that 
meeting  of  the  Regional  Board  and  such  substitute 
may  attend  the  meeting  subject  to  the  approval  of 
his  appointment  by  the  Chairman  of  the  Regional 
Board.  The  substitute  shall  have  all  the  rights  and 
powers  of  a member  in  respect  of  that  meeting  and 
shall  receive  fees  and  allowances  admissible  to 
members  of  the  Rerional  Board  but  his  presence 
would  not  relieve  the  member  of  his  obligation 
under  Regulation  10(6)(i)  of  the  en^loyees’  State 
Insurance  (General)  Regulations,  1950”. 

I.  D.  BAJAJ
